Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the dynamics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 598. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 344 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_598(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 598. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^344(S, F) is trivial. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 407. QED.
